### Handover after Phase 12 (2026-11-09)

- **State:** Phase 12 is complete but for 8.6b, which still waits for the owner (retried at 12.6: 62.15 KB with Phase 12 merged in, the same one conflict in `FormModels`' parameters with both sides kept, against ADR-0146's 60 KB). Nothing else is half-done.
  - **The library (12.1, ADR-0214 to ADR-0217):** option lists with their use, themes over a theme in code with a token editor and contrast checks, email layouts with a preview and a test send, nine built-in templates, saved forms and groups.
  - **Settings and access (12.4a, ADR-0218):** one settings screen for every section with host locks and per-site values; the access matrix and Permissions… on folders and forms.
  - **Activity (12.4b, ADR-0219):** Deliveries with retry and the audit log with its export.
  - **Forms as files (12.5, ADR-0220):** Replace from a file…, and the file mirror with its health check.
  - **The phase review (12.6, ADR-0221):** see the WORKLOG line above and `docs/ux/review-12.md`.
- **The review:** each part's own (review-12.3.md for the templates), the three design skills on every part, and 12.6's walk of Phase 12's screens in twelve looks, before and after its fixes.
- **Wiring sweep:** taken from the code by a reader over `b74cfedb..6aae2234`, each name checked against product code and tests; what it found is fixed in 605be3c.
  - **Host options and settings:** every setting a section serves is a field of its screen or host-only, and product code outside the settings module reads it (`SettingsWiringTests`); `Definitions:Mirror` and `Definitions:Folder` are read by the file mirror and validated through `FileStoreRoot` (`FormBuilderOptionsTests`, `FormMirrorTests`).
  - **Endpoints** (management; each has its backoffice caller and its row in the permission matrix): `settings/{section}` GET and PUT; `access`, `access/folder/{key}` and `access/form/{key}`, GET and PUT; `delivery`, a mail's and a run's details (`delivery/email/{id}`, `delivery/run/{id}`) and their retries; `audit` and `audit/export`; `form/{key}/replace/check` and `form/{key}/replace`; the library's option list, theme, email layout and template endpoints.
  - **Notifications, audit and codes:** `form.replaced`, `email.retried`, `access.grantChanged`, `settings.saved`, `audit.exported`, `theme.deleted`, `emailLayout.deleted`, each asserted; every new problem code raised by its controller with a test (`duplicateGroup` and `administrators` among them).
  - **Health checks:** "Forms as files" (`MirrorHealthCheck`) with its action through Umbraco's collection, and in the setup's list of checks.
  - **Jobs:** `FormMirrorJob`, every minute on the scheduling server, gated on the mirror's switch, the runtime level and pending migrations, and catching a failed pass.
  - **Caches:** option lists, access, published forms and themes forget locally at once, then tell every server (`LibraryThemeCacheTests`).
  - **Found and fixed by the sweep:** a theme stale in the same request, the mirror's problem keys with a capital letter, the unasserted audits and codes, unused activity words, and untested mirror paths (605be3c).
- **The gate:** on `ad8b295` in a throwaway clone (ADR-0117, ADR-0194): client 5,805, unit 8,636 (16 skipped), integration 2,157, browser 513, all in `ci-local`, 3,415 s, the backoffice's and the runtime's budgets in its client stage. Five earlier runs failed, each fixed at its cause: the API tests' caller read the audit log's CSV export as JSON (`ebc17d5`); the settings test's signing secret by a reference no host holds met the Teams test in another worker (`c2b4486`, the `site-wide` project); the 300-field budget at 104 ms, as Phase 11's own commit did on the same day, the canvas's scroller painting its whole view at each change (`939d532`); a long name in the tree lying over its row's "..." (`bd71f29`); and the narrowest journey counting the tree's new ellipsis (`ad8b295`).
- **Coverage:** Core 97.7% (32,653/33,414 lines), Web 97.7% (9,749/9,983), from the collector's report of unit 8,636 and integration 2,157 (`node scripts/coverage.mjs --run`). `secret-scan --tracked` clean (3,603 files), `term-guard --tracked` skipped by design (0 patterns), the name scan 0 hits.

- **Next:** 13.1, the first step of Phase 13: privacy, retention and compliance.
- **Gotchas:**
  - **A visually hidden control is still a control,** and form styles that size controls size it too: the utility's rules are `!important` (ADR-0221).
  - **`uui-box` lays out its own content:** a grid's gap on the box itself spaces nothing; put a block inside it.
  - **Bring a tree row to the sidebar's middle before hovering it, and try the hover and the click again together** (`support/tree.ts`, the walk's `rowAction`): a tree still reading rows moves the row from under the pointer.
  - **The forms tree's label keeps Umbraco's `#label` id:** without it a long name runs over the row's "..." and the click opens the form (ADR-0221).
  - **A spec that saves a site-wide setting or grant goes in `specs/site-wide`,** which runs after the specs that read them; the browser specs share one host.
  - **A scroller's own background is painted with its contents:** a solid colour on a long, changing view repaints all of it at every change; let the host's colour show through and keep the layer with `will-change: scroll-position` (ADR-0221).
  - **The designer's budget sits close to this machine's drawing:** compare with an older commit in a clone before calling a slower run a regression (ADR-0186, ADR-0221).
  - **The walk runs with two workers:** Umbraco 17's SQLite hangs under four.
  - **A fresh demo needs `Demo:Seed` false, its own port, and Umbraco's sender with a display name;** a host without content or people has neither ready line, so name the ready page and lines (`support/host.ts`).
  - **A key in `jfbKeyValue` may hold no capital letter:** build keys from lowercase parts.

  - **Edit project files with the Edit tool:** heredocs and JavaScript template literals eat the backslashes of `..\..\` paths and of `\u` escapes.
  - **check-text catches what the file tools write:** a `…`, `“` or `Ü` in a spec or test goes in as an escape (the hook refused two this step); run the escape script on new files before staging.
  - **A look spec signs in once per test:** a second `loginAs` on a signed-in page waits for a login form that never comes.
  - **`sed -i` in Git Bash rewrites a CRLF file with LF:** restore a `.ps1`'s line endings with Node.
  - **Umbraco.AI providers are found by attribute:** a host that references a provider's assembly lists it unless a composer excludes it (`builder.AIProviders().Exclude<T>()`).

  1. a web font with an escape outside quotes could still load from another host (16.1's X3 held declarations only);
  2. any socket error counted as no mail server, so a connection reset while a mail went out gave back its attempt and could send it twice (ADR-0237 amended);
  3. Esc in the entries list dropped the focus to the page, where the list's keys stop;
  4. the preview showed a content action's refusal as a failure to load, or turned its switch back silently;
  5. past the limiter's bound an address's upload share was counted in its overflow window but never checked there (ADR-0148);
  6. a duplicate, copied actions, a saved template and a form from a template carried a secret typed as itself on from an older draft (ADR-0236);
  7. a published version this server cannot read was parsed again, and logged, on every post (ADR-0237);
  8. the install test never recognised its own site under PowerShell 7 (a date in its record);
  9. missing tests: content action rights beyond the section, a run-order-only change, the AI add-on's logging, the phone column at 375 px;
  10. the review document's missing after-shot.

- **Gotchas:**
  - **A walk step that reloads the backoffice needs the view first:** a reload can take longer than an inner five-second wait; wait for the view, then for what is in it.
  - **A tree's rows come a page at a time:** the shared host's root holds every test's forms, so a row made late in a run can sit on the second page; read the tree with `shownRow` or `loadedIn` (`support/tree.ts`), which look for "Load more" only once the first page is there.
  - **A dashed line around rounded corners is slow to draw on this machine's graphics chip:** keep dashed lines on square corners (`canvas-rings.test.ts`); to find what a paint costs, split Event Timing's marks (delay, handlers, wait for the paint) and trace the GPU process before changing code.
  - **A test's browser draws everything for the first time:** Playwright starts each run with a new profile, so its graphics chip compiles a program the first time it draws each kind of thing (a blurred shadow, a dashed rounded line), which a real browser keeps. A slow first interaction that is fast in a second page of the same browser is that; the designer's budget measures it apart (ADR-0243).
  - **The header's search can take a click before it is wired:** no dialog opens; `openSearch` (`support/search.ts`) clicks again until its tab shows.
  - **Reloading right after a dialog closes can cut off its request:** the publish answers after the dialog closes; wait for its response.
  - **Backticks in a bash double-quoted string run as commands:** a comment with a code mark lost its word; write such text through a script file.
  - **`ContentActionSettings` is a class, not a record:** no `with`; build its test values with named arguments.
  - **The axe flags of a moment:** a toast over a card or a sticky footer over the last radio reads as a target too small; look at what lies on top before changing sizes.

### Handover after Phase 17 (2026-11-13)

- **State:** Phase 17 is complete but for 8.6b, which still waits for the owner (retried at 17.1: 61.67 KB with Phase 17 merged in, the same two server-side conflicts, against ADR-0146's 60 KB). Nothing else is half-done.
  - **The mark (17.1, ADR-0245):** `assets/logo.svg`, a form card in Ligata's blue with two fields cut out and a marigold check, the one-colour mark, the backoffice's `icon-jfb`, and PNGs at 512 to 64 px from `scripts/icons.mjs`; `assets/brand.md` says why the default theme keeps its own accent.
  - **The documentation (17.2a, 17.2b, ADR-0244):** the pages under `docs/` for editors, site owners and developers, the README and the NuGet readme, the generated configuration reference, Learn more from one map of 19 topics, the link check and the Markdown renderer.
  - **Screenshots (17.3, ADR-0244):** 19 pictures in `docs/screenshots/` from `scripts/screenshots.mjs`, re-encoded without a changed pixel by `scripts/png.mjs`.
- **The review:** two readers read the pages as a new editor (29 questions) and as a new developer (34), and a second reading checked the answers (17.1's line above). A third reader went over Phase 17's code and its uncommitted work for what it added and how each part is wired. Eleven findings, each checked against the code and fixed:
  1. four browser tests still expected the old words and units: the block dialog's "Open document types", More options (4), "the site's clock" and Largest file in bytes (64ba4972);
  2. the letter-case test of the link check would have failed on Linux, where a wrongly spelt file is simply missing; a case mismatch is now found before a missing file on any file system (821f98ba);
  3. the PNG test assumed unfiltered rows never pack larger than filtered ones, which its own picture disproved; it now checks that the smaller of the two is kept and that both give the same pixels (544a1f8b);
  4. the screenshots' second version counted the history's rows before its list had loaded, so a slow demo would be published again; it waits for a row now (544a1f8b);
  5. the alias row put its name on a `code` element, which screen readers do not read, and a long alias pushed the view sideways on a phone; the row is a group named by its label and the alias wraps (542bce06);
  6. the German help of the team's addresses read as if it meant the user (1bde127f);
  7. the PHP signature sample's fallback to the current time had no test (f88bba24);
  8. `png.mjs` dropped a `tRNS`, `gAMA` or `iCCP` chunk, which changes pixels or colours; a PNG carrying such a chunk is now left as it is (544a1f8b);

### Handover after Phase 19 (2026-11-14)

- **State:** Phase 19 is complete. 8.6b waited for the owner's word on the runtime's budget (retried at 19.1: 61.71 KB with Phase 19 merged in, the same two server-side conflicts in `FormModels.cs` and `RenderingWords.cs`, against ADR-0146's 60 KB); on 2026-11-14 the owner raised the budget to 100 KB (ADR-0251, cddc4bb5), so 8.6b is open again and comes next. Nothing else is half-done. Two lines now: `main` for Umbraco 17 (17.0.0-alpha.1.dev between releases) and `v18/main` for Umbraco 18 (18.0.0-alpha.0), both on origin.
  - **The 18 line (ADR-0250):** the ranges, the OpenAPI port with its rules, the backoffice's tree adapters, the obsoletions replaced; `docs/` on `v18/main` names Umbraco 18 and says how a site moves from 17 to 18.
  - **On both lines, from `main`:** the integration tests release the file providers a test's host was handed; the webhook fixture names its server role; the form workspace's context ends once; the form header's name and state wait for room where Umbraco leaves less than the name's 6rem (which only Umbraco 18.3 brings about); the browser tests' stall detector counts Umbraco 18's locked database, and a sign-in it stalls is made once more; the install test learns the line; the version and package checks read the line's major.
- **The review:** a reader went over the 18 port's code (the OpenAPI registration and rules, the tree adapters, the conditions, the replaced obsoletions) against the code that calls it in the backoffice package 18. No material defect: the window arithmetic holds at every edge (a target at the start, the end, on a page boundary, a level smaller than a page, an empty level, a target that is gone, a folders-only list), and the three documents and both clients match the 17 line's but for the differences ADR-0250 names. Five low points, each settled (aedb7870, e655a58d):
  1. the read-only rule ran before the generator puts in references, so a get-only member whose type has a schema of its own would have marked that shared schema read-only; it now marks only a value written in place;
  2. a get-only member set through the constructor would have counted as read-only; such a member is left alone;
  3. four rules (read-only, extensible problems, optional bodies, the declarations' references) had no unit test though the ADR said each had; each has one now, and each fails against a tamper;
  4. the public declarations named a generic type of another library unlike the document; such a type is refused;
  5. a window around a tree item was read 100 items at a time; it reads 500, the endpoints' most.
- **The wiring:** no setting key, job, endpoint or permission was added. The OpenAPI rules run in every document's registration (the snapshot tests read the documents through Umbraco's `IOpenApiDocumentProvider`, and the management test asserts one keyed mark for `jfb`); `readLevel` serves both trees (`tree-paging.test.ts`, the library's browser specs on 18); the release of the providers runs after every integration test (`TestHostReleaseTests`); the context's single end is held by the designer's browser test.
- **The gates:** `v18/main` e54d894c passed in 5,385 s, its browser projects on their one rerun (ADR-0011): the first run's host found one write locked at 22:38:50, and two member sign-ins after it got no answer within their tests' time; the rerun passed all 555 on fresh hosts. - **Gotchas:**
  - **A leak shows as late requests timing out:** sample the test host's working set; a heap dump at about 3 GB and `gcroot` on an old and a middle instance find the root (the dump's path must be a Windows path).
  - **Umbraco 18.3 gives a workspace's header slot `flex-basis: 0`:** the view tabs take their whole width and the header what is left (17 folds tabs); the npm package 18.1.0 does not show it, the served 18.3.1 does. Read the backoffice the host serves (`Umbraco.Cms.StaticAssets` in the NuGet cache), not only the npm package.
  - **Umbraco 18 on SQLite stalls sign-ins under the browser tests' load:** without the shared cache any worker's write overtakes a sign-in's read of its user, and the request does not answer for minutes with nothing logged; a Playwright trace (`trace.zip`, its `.network` files) shows the request without an answer. The harness signs in once more after 20 s, a member as a backoffice user, each under the one sign-in lock, and gives the test those 20 s back.
  - **`net::ERR_NO_BUFFER_SPACE` in a trace is Windows, not the host:** the browser got no socket at that moment, so a page's script never arrived and its form posted without the runtime; a later page of the same test loaded it at once. One test of one 18 gate met it.
  - **Hide rather than squeeze:** an input squeezed to a sliver crowds a neighbour below axe's 24 px of clear space; parts without room wait out of sight (`visibility: hidden`, which keeps the layout and so cannot feed back into the measure).
  - **Umbraco 18 sees a test host as a single server,** where 17 saw no role; a test about server roles names the role it means.
  - **Umbraco 18's `UpdateAndPublishAsync` reports the save and the publish apart:** a refused value is saved as a draft, and `Success` is false while the save's own status says `Success`.
  - **A context may be ended twice:** an add-on's registry ends a workspace's contexts as Umbraco does; `destroy()` must end once.
  - **The 18 worktree needs its own `npm ci` in `tests/e2e`** before the install test's checks run there.
  - **Umbraco 17 opens the Recycle Bin at `/jfb-recycle-bin/edit/null`, 18 at `/jfb-recycle-bin`:** a check of the address accepts both.
  - **A wrapped Markdown line that starts with a number and a full stop starts a numbered list:** wrap before the number.
  - **Gate one line at a time:** both lines share the browser projects' ports; `node scripts/gate-clone.mjs run <commit>` gates either line's commit, as the clone holds both branches.
